fine awareness, disclosed knowledge, divinatory report, and believing assent distinguish modes of access to reality. The hidden is not collapsed into the visible; rather, the source and reliability of each report become the issue.","ayah_refs":["69:38","69:39","69:41","69:43","69:49","69:42"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: The oath spans sight, insight, and certain truth","parent_semantic_invariant":"The assertion is secured across sensory visibility, inward recognition, sworn testimony, and knowledge from which doubt has been removed.","parent_surprising_reach":"Evidence is materialized as blood-trace, shield, stitched hide, road-mark, and bright stone, so seeing becomes a practice of reading surfaces and protections.","key":"C","name":"Sworn division and distributed testimony","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An oath allocates responsibility and binds speakers to a claim before an audience.","active_motifs":"إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); يمين مقسومة على أهلها (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); بال مقسم بين وجوه الأمر (`quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`); هدنة قسامة (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`); يمين الحلف (`quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); استغراق النفي (`quranic:root_000017:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ي م ن`: 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)","synthesis":"Division into shares, oath, deliberative apportionment, truce, sworn pledge, voiced statement, and all-inclusive 'anyone' place testimony in a social procedure. Responsibility is distributed, but the uttered claim binds every participating voice.","ayah_refs":["69:38","69:45","69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44","69:47"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Authorized speech descends through a trusted carrier","parent_semantic_invariant":"A valid message has an originating authority, an ordered descent, an honored bearer, and a speech act that preserves rather than invents its charge.","parent_surprising_reach":"Transmission is rendered as release, carried saying, measured gait, repeated descent, assigned rank, guest-provision, and a shipmaster who keeps the course.","key":"A","name":"Messenger, carried saying, and measured delivery","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A bearer receives an utterance and conveys it with deliberate pace and fidelity.","active_motifs":"الإرسال والانبعاث (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); الرفق والتؤدة (`quranic:root_000563:B004/m01`); المراسلة والمسايرة (`quranic:root_000563:B008/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); الشرف والجود المحمود (`quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`); العزيز الذي يكرم عليك (`quranic:root_001294:B010/m01`); رباني الملاحين (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر س ل`: 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)","synthesis":"Dispatch, messenger, carried statement, easy gait, deliberation, paired performance, voiced saying, honor, dear entrusted object, and captaincy create a reliable delivery scene. The bearer is valued because movement, speech, and custodial role remain aligned.","ayah_refs":["69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44","69:43","69:52"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Poetry and divination are rival production systems","parent_semantic_invariant":"Poetic composition and divinatory report have their own materials, marks, meters, and social agents, but they do not explain the message's source.","parent_surprising_reach":"Poetry's root reaches hair, vegetation, barley, undergarment, blood-mark, star-name, and segmented meter; divination reaches hidden news, service, and tribal title.","key":"A","name":"Metered poem, poet, and segmented line","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A craftsperson composes measured language whose units can be divided and compared.","active_motifs":"قريض وشاعر (`quranic:root_000798:B007/m01`); كلام وشعر مقطع (`quranic:root_001240:B022/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); تجزئة الشيء وطائفته (`quranic:root_000133:B001/m01`); إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ب ع ض`: 69:44 (بَعْضَ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)","synthesis":"Poetry, poet, segmented verse, clipped speech, utterance, fluent speaker, part, and division describe a made verbal object. The denial identifies a genuine production system but excludes it as the source of this saying.","ayah_refs":["69:41","69:46","69:40","69:42","69:44","69:38"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Poetry and divination are rival production systems","parent_semantic_invariant":"Poetic composition and divinatory report have their own materials, marks, meters, and social agents, but they do not explain the message's source.","parent_surprising_reach":"Poetry's root reaches hair, vegetation, barley, undergarment, blood-mark, star-name, and segmented meter; divination reaches hidden news, service, and tribal title.","key":"B","name":"Fine awareness, sign, and bodily slogan","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Subtle perception is externalized as a sign worn close to body or displayed in ritual and conflict.","active_motifs":"شعار يلاصق الجسد (`quranic:root_000798:B004/m01`); علم دقيق وفطنة (`quranic:root_000798:B005/m01`); علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)","synthesis":"Undergarment or inward slogan, fine awareness, ritual or battle mark, guiding sign, and spoken mention move between inner apprehension and public emblem. The scene shows why poetic and prophetic language can be confused: both make the barely perceived socially visible.","ayah_refs":["69:41","69:43","69:49","69:42","69:48"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Poetry and divination are rival production systems","parent_semantic_invariant":"Poetic composition and divinatory report have their own materials, marks, meters, and social agents, but they do not explain the message's source.","parent_surprising_reach":"Poetry's root reaches hair, vegetation, barley, undergarment, blood-mark, star-name, and segmented meter; divination reaches hidden news, service, and tribal title.","key":"D","name":"Soothsayer, hidden information, and assigned service","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A specialist claims access to concealed reports and may act as an attendant within a social group.","active_motifs":"الكاهن المتكهن بالأخبار الخفية (`quranic:root_001327:B001/m01`); الكاهن القائم بأمر الرجل (`quranic:root_001327:B002/m01`); الكاهنان لقب لقبيلتين (`quranic:root_001327:B003/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); أسماء مخصوصة منقولة (`quranic:root_000798:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ه ن`: 69:42 (كَاهِنٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ)","synthesis":"Hidden-news diviner, appointed attendant, tribal title, knowledge, distinguishing mark, and transferred proper names define the social institution of soothsaying. The denial separates revelation from both speculative access and inherited office.","ayah_refs":["69:42","69:43","69:49","69:41"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Poetry and divination are rival production systems","parent_semantic_invariant":"Poetic composition and divinatory report have their own materials, marks, meters, and social agents, but they do not explain the message's source.","parent_surprising_reach":"Poetry's root reaches hair, vegetation, barley, undergarment, blood-mark, star-name, and segmented meter; divination reaches hidden news, service, and tribal title.","key":"E","name":"Particle, definition, and formal language","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Speech is organized by small grammatical operators and explicit delimitation of meaning.","active_motifs":"حرف رب وربما (`quranic:root_000532:B015/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ)","synthesis":"The particle `rubba`, logical definition, and naming form a formal language layer beneath extended utterance. These devices organize reference and scope without themselves identifying the message's source.","ayah_refs":["69:43","69:52","69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Fabrication is exposed as attributed and circulating speech","parent_semantic_invariant":"False discourse is produced by inventing words, assigning them to another, circulating them socially, and resisting the marks that disclose their source.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lie reaches a failed military charge, stopping animal, vanished milk, painted cloth, rumor, logical definition, and the act of cutting speech into misleading fragments.","key":"A","name":"Invented saying and false attribution","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A speaker composes what was not said and attaches it to an authority.","active_motifs":"قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); اجترار القول إلى النفس (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); كلام وشعر مقطع (`quranic:root_001240:B022/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)","synthesis":"Fabrication, appropriation of a saying, lie, accusation of lying, and clipped or segmented speech define authorship fraud. The counterfactual is precise because both production and attribution must occur for the offense to exist.","ayah_refs":["69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44","69:49","69:46"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Fabrication is exposed as attributed and circulating speech","parent_semantic_invariant":"False discourse is produced by inventing words, assigning them to another, circulating them socially, and resisting the marks that disclose their source.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lie reaches a failed military charge, stopping animal, vanished milk, painted cloth, rumor, logical definition, and the act of cutting speech into misleading fragments.","key":"B","name":"Circulating report, name, and public reputation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Speech detaches from its first speaker and becomes a social current of praise, blame, or rumor.","active_motifs":"كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); هدية تطلب المكافأة (`quranic:root_001294:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ)","synthesis":"Fluent speaker, circulating talk, negotiation, mention, renown, name, favorable reputation, and gift seeking reward through praise form a publicity system. False attribution gains force only if this circulation carries it.","ayah_refs":["69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44","69:48","69:52"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Fabrication is exposed as attributed and circulating speech","parent_semantic_invariant":"False discourse is produced by inventing words, assigning them to another, circulating them socially, and resisting the marks that disclose their source.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lie reaches a failed military charge, stopping animal, vanished milk, painted cloth, rumor, logical definition, and the act of cutting speech into misleading fragments.","key":"D","name":"Deceptive surface and resistant inner self","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An outer pattern or covering misstates what lies beneath while the self sustains the deception.","active_motifs":"النفس الكذوب (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); الكذابة ثوب يكذب بحاله (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); حجب الحق (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); غلظ الحافة ووصل الشقتين (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); قول في النفس لم يظهر (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); القول اعتقاد ومذهب (`quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ)","synthesis":"Deceptive self, multicolored cloth, covering, covered truth, stitched surface, inward statement, adopted doctrine, and formal definition distinguish outer sign from inner commitment. Exposure requires reading both layers.","ayah_refs":["69:49","69:50","69:38","69:39","69:40","69:41","69:42","69:44"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Reminder makes an inward trace publicly legible","parent_semantic_invariant":"Remembering preserves content in the heart, reactivates it through speech or sign, and leaves a durable mark that can guide later recognition.","parent_surprising_reach":"The reminder becomes a legal deed, male hardness, honor, battle-sign, road-marker, inscribed edge, prayer bead, and named star or mountain.","key":"A","name":"Retention, recollection, and active prompting","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Stored content is brought back into attention and offered as a prompt to conduct.","active_motifs":"استحضار الشيء بعد النسيان أو مع الحفظ (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`); جعل النفس في وقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ء م ن`: 69:41 (تُؤْمِنُ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ)","synthesis":"Retention against forgetting, reminder, knowledge, assent, and self-guarding form the cognitive-behavioral sequence. The reminder succeeds when recovered content becomes anticipatory protection.","ayah_refs":["69:42","69:48","69:43","69:49","69:41"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Reminder makes an inward trace publicly legible","parent_semantic_invariant":"Remembering preserves content in the heart, reactivates it through speech or sign, and leaves a durable mark that can guide later recognition.","parent_surprising_reach":"The reminder becomes a legal deed, male hardness, honor, battle-sign, road-marker, inscribed edge, prayer bead, and named star or mountain.","key":"B","name":"Spoken mention, honor, and durable deed","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Memory is externalized as repeated name, public reputation, or written evidence of a right.","active_motifs":"جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); ذكر الحق صك ووثيقة حق (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ)","synthesis":"Mention, renown, deed of right, name, favorable reputation, and identifying mark carry memory outside an individual mind. They let later observers recognize a person, claim, or path.","ayah_refs":["69:42","69:48","69:52","69:43","69:49"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Reminder makes an inward trace publicly legible","parent_semantic_invariant":"Remembering preserves content in the heart, reactivates it through speech or sign, and leaves a durable mark that can guide later recognition.","parent_surprising_reach":"The reminder becomes a legal deed, male hardness, honor, battle-sign, road-marker, inscribed edge, prayer bead, and named star or mountain.","key":"C","name":"Ritual sign, bodily mark, and counted remembrance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A visible or tactile token cues communal identity and repeated devotional action.","active_motifs":"علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); شق ظاهر في الشفة العليا (`quranic:root_001040:B004/m01`); خرز التسبيح (`quranic:root_000666:B006/m01`); الذكر خلاف الأنثى (`quranic:root_000516:B001/m01`); صلابة الذكر وحدته وشدته (`quranic:root_000516:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)","synthesis":"Ritual or battle sign, guiding mark, split lip, counting beads, male classification, and hard masculine material show remembrance embodied in tokens. Mark and repetition keep identity available to eye, hand, and community.","ayah_refs":["69:41","69:43","69:49","69:52","69:42","69:48"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Sacred elevation resolves into naming and praise","parent_semantic_invariant":"Divine transcendence is articulated through lordship, greatness, elevated name, purification from defect, worship, and grateful movement.","parent_surprising_reach":"Praise reaches prayer, voluntary worship, counted beads, running and swimming, strong leather garments, holy locations, cloud-canopy, and the leader of sailors.","key":"D","name":"Purification, worship, and expansive motion","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Praise clears defect from the named one and releases the worshipper into repeated devotional movement.","active_motifs":"العبادة بالتسبيح والصلاة (`quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`); التنزيه والتبرئة (`quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`); السبح في الجري والعوم (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); السعة للذهاب والمعاش (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`); خرز التسبيح (`quranic:root_000666:B006/m01`); سباح الجلود والكساء (`quranic:root_000666:B007/m01`); سبوحة الموضع (`quranic:root_000666:B008/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)","synthesis":"Prayer, purification from defect, swimming or running, freedom to move for livelihood, counting beads, strong ritual clothing, sacred place, and spoken mention turn glorification into embodied practice. Motion and material token prevent praise from remaining an unperformed abstraction.","ayah_refs":["69:52","69:42","69:48"]},{"parent_channel":"P3: Quantity is formed by part, share, one, and few","parent_semantic_invariant":"A whole is analyzed through partition, allotted share, discrete unit, small amount, and singular identity.","parent_surprising_reach":"Quantification reaches water allocation, blood-oath distribution, large jars, mountain peaks, cloth-folding, tremor, Sunday, and isolated individuals.","key":"C","name":"Fewness, summit, jar, load, and tremor","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A root of small quantity unfolds into upper point, containing vessel, lifted burden, and a body's slight but visible disturbance.","active_motifs":"القِلَّة والضآلة (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); قُلَّة الشيء ورأسه (`quranic:root_001251:B002/m01`); القُلَّة الجرة الكبيرة (`quranic:root_001251:B003/m01`); الإقلال والاستقلال حملا ونهوضا (`quranic:root_001251:B004/m01`); القِلُّ رعدة واضطراب (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); حسن موزع في الوجه (`quranic:root_001226:B001/m01`); طي القسامي أول الثوب (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ل ل`: 69:41 (قَلِيلًا), 69:42 (قَلِيلًا); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)","synthesis":"Fewness, peak, large jar, carrying and departure, tremor, well-proportioned face, and first fold of cloth show quantity through scale, elevation, containment, motion, and form. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
